# All bass multi-effects — comparison

[Guitar](ALL-GUITAR.md) · [Bass](ALL-BASS.md) · [Vocal](ALL-VOCAL.md) · [Category](MULTI-EFFECTS.md) · [Repository](README.md)

122 recorded bass-focused or explicitly documented bass-capable multi-effects: 28 bass-focused and 94 shared models. This compares actual model records, not only bass-branded units. Capability evidence is listed below; it does not establish equal low-end retention or every effect working equally well on bass.

These are **comparisons of the recorded catalogue**, not exhaustive market coverage or fresh stock certification. Organized 2026-10-16 from the linked source records; their original technical/price dates, firmware, revision and access limits still apply. Every model remains partial. **? means unverified, not No.** Read the complete linked record for sources and conflicting evidence; this view does not silently resolve those conflicts.

K/F/B = independently adjustable rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons; toe and power switches are additional unless explicitly stated. Body axes and mass qualifications are retained. Color does not prove touch; USB audio does not prove bus power; IRs, native NAM, converted NAM and proprietary captures differ.

PR-M pp.14–17; PR-S<br>**Prime — category-count qualifications:** The current product list prints these category headers: HeadRush amps **53**, ReValver amps **44**, ReValver cabinet microphones **31**; vocal **7**, overdrive **16**, distortion/fuzz **11**, EQ **9**, compressor **6**, delay **14**, reverb **7**, chorus **7**, phaser/flanger **10**, vib/trem/rotary **6**, wah/filter **8**, synth **4**, rhythmic **2**, pitch **7**, volume/dynamics **5**, utility **3**. These are **published header counts, not a reconciled installed-library census**. Overdrive visibly lists **18 names beneath its 16 header**; pitch lists eight rows with **Octaves duplicated**. Firmware 5.1 adds Graphic Bass EQ, but the displayed EQ list needs reconciliation. Do not silently sum these into a definitive current total. Cabinet/mic/IR combinations are separate from independent effects. PR-P, PR-R | **Memory:** FAQ states **50 preloaded factory rigs**; firmware-current installed content and numerical total rig/user storage ceiling unverified. Rigs, block presets, scenes, setlists, loops and captures are distinct objects. MIDI program numbers 1–128 are not proof of only 128 stored rigs. PR-F, PR-M | **Synth / acoustic:** Current Prime list explicitly includes **Hybrid Synth, Electric Piano and DB-33 Organ**; Ambi-Verb is also classified under Synth. **Acoustic Sim** and Acoustic Pre are separate EQ-category entries. PR-P<br>**Vocal:** Antares Auto-Tune, De-Esser, Vocal Distortion, Doubler, Harmony, Stutter and Vocoder are separately listed; mic path and routing qualifications above apply. PR-P, PR-S | **IR / captures:** Third-party IR loading, 1024/2048-sample processing; FAQ accepts **WAV/AIFF up to 192 kHz/32-bit** and two IRs in a block. Processing length differs from accepted source-file rate/depth. Exact conversion rules/slot ceiling unresolved. Proprietary onboard **Amp Cloner**, HeadRush/ReValver clones and SuperClones are separate from NAM; SuperClone assembly is a desktop software workflow. PR-F, PR-M, PR-R<br>**Native / converted NAM:** **Native NAM A1/A2**, not a conversion-only claim. 5.1 documented one A1 Standard/Lite/Feather/Nano or A2 Full/Lite per rig; **5.2 allows two A2 Full, or four A2 Lite across two NAM blocks**, including doubling modes. Do not apply the newer A2 limits to every A1 architecture. PR-R |

Noon is flat; mid cut/boost frequencies differ. — Evidence: [Manual p4](https://www.tech21nyc.com/t21manuals/BassFlyRig_v2_OM.pdf)<br>**Effects / mix controls:** Octave and fuzz combine or work separately; MIX spans dry-only through 50/50 to wet-only. Q minimum makes RANGE a high-cut tone control. Chorus knob changes amount/depth without a separate speed control. — Evidence: [Manual pp5–6](https://www.tech21nyc.com/t21manuals/BassFlyRig_v2_OM.pdf) | **Factory / user memory:** Two channel control pairs are documented; programmable factory/user/total preset storage unverified. — Evidence: [Manual pp3–4](https://www.tech21nyc.com/t21manuals/BassFlyRig_v2_OM.pdf) | **Synth / acoustic:** Octafilter provides synth-style tones; octave divider is monophonic. Acoustic simulation unverified. — Evidence: [Product](https://www.tech21nyc.com/products/flyrig/bass-fly-rig-v2/); [Manual p5](https://www.tech21nyc.com/t21manuals/BassFlyRig_v2_OM.pdf) | **IR / NAM / capture:** Analog cabinet simulation; defeat affects quarter-inch only, not XLR. External downstream IR suggestion does not establish onboard user IR, native/converted NAM or capture loading. — Evidence: [Product](https://www.tech21nyc.com/products/flyrig/bass-fly-rig-v2/); [Manual p4](https://www.tech21nyc.com/t21manuals/BassFlyRig_v2_OM.pdf) |

[Product](https://www.behringer.com/en/products/0709-AAC), [Manual](https://images.thomann.de/pics/atg/atgdata/document/manual/163587_manual_engl.pdf)<br>**Processing / post-effect choices:** [Manufacturer manual v1.2,December2005,table6.1,p14](https://images.thomann.de/pics/atg/atgdata/document/manual/163587_manual_engl.pdf) enumerates **15**:VCF+Flanger,Delay+Chorus,StereoDelay,Delay/Loop,Reverb,Ambience,VoiceBox,Ultrabass,RotaryCab,Phaser,Flanger,Chorus,StereoChorus,Synth,MIDISynth. The text advertises16groups;the missing sixteenth remains unresolved. Do not silently reconcile it with bypass or a pre-effect.<br>**Processing / pre-effects:** Table6.2,p13 lists MIDIWah,P-Funk’n,AutoWah. MIDIWah excludes the other two;these choices are separate from the post-effect table. Compressor anddenoiser operate independently of the multi-effects section. Exact aggregate ceiling remains unverified.<br>**Processing / latency:** [Manufacturer manual v1.2,December2005,p18](https://images.thomann.de/pics/atg/atgdata/document/manual/163587_manual_engl.pdf) specifies approximately **5ms LineIn→LineOut**,31.250kHz internal sample rate and24-bit converters. This is the published path value,not a new measurement or USB-audio specification. | **Factory / user memory:** **125 factory-programmed, editable/storable locations, 25 × 5**; factory originals restorable to the same locations, not a separate additional user bank. [Manual](https://images.thomann.de/pics/atg/atgdata/document/manual/163587_manual_engl.pdf)<br>**Factory / user memory:** [Manufacturer manual v1.2,December2005,p10](https://images.thomann.de/pics/atg/atgdata/document/manual/163587_manual_engl.pdf) describes restoring the original factory preset for each location and restoring all factory presets. The125 editable locations therefore hold factory-programmed sounds;do not add a second125-slot user bank.<br>**Memory / save:** Hold the destination preset button about2seconds to overwrite it;bank selection allows saving elsewhere. Selecting another preset discards unsaved edits.<br>**Memory / restoration:** Holding both arrows until Pr recalls the location’s original sound;save it to retain the restoration. D+E during startup,then both arrows after CL,restores all factory sounds and overwrites user saves. Manual points to MIDI SysEx backup in8.4.1;this does not establish an editor application. | **Synth / acoustic simulation:** Three-voice synth with 9 variants; MIDI synth separate. ULTRABASS adds lower octave. Acoustic voicing includes piezo/magnetic processing; electric-to-acoustic simulation **?**. MIDISynth has its own MIDI-accessible reverb;not proof of freely assignable extra reverb on every patch. | **IR / NAM / capture:** User IR import, native NAM, converted NAM, capture **?**; factory cabinet simulations alone do not establish import |

| [Audota AME-200](AUDOTA.md#ame-200) | **Audio/control ports:** One6.35mm instrument input;two6.35mm L/R outs;separate3.5mm stereo phones;external EXP;USB-C;9VDC. No analog aux,mic/phantom,FX-loop or DIN/TRS MIDI in complete inventory M Manufacturer specs additionally publish input2.2MΩ, output510Ω and headphone32Ω. M4,M12,R | **Power:** **Power: universal100–240 V AC,50/60 Hz,40 W**, supplied IEC cable; no DC pedal-adapter polarity applies. In standard mode computer-return3/4 are disabled; reamp mode enables them for instrument/mic reamping and mutes the physical inputs. Windows ASIO driver is separate from the editor. Do not label the older English headline the latest known firmware. The newer version also renames FBE/Freeman models to Forge; old names in an earlier guide are not additional models. R,J<br>**Looper / live:** **Looper expanded from the product page's60 seconds to three minutes in1.7.53.** Three polysynths and MIDI-over-USB support were added in that same release. The June2026 update adds8 amps,8 cabinets and15 effects; these are update increments, not the complete library and not to be added again to an updated model list. Scene/snapshot functions, songs/setlists,tuner and delay/reverb spillover are documented; no measured global latency is asserted. R,J,P |

PG-M,PG-Y,PG-P<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** Line 6 specifies **up to30 m/100 ft line-of-sight** and about **seven hours transmitter playing time**, both manufacturer ratings rather than independent tests. The floorboard needs its own supply. RF antennas are above the expression-pedal toe area. Wireless reliability depends on RF conditions; do not cover the antenna or assume arbitrary crowded2.4 GHz conditions meet the rated range. PG-W<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** GUITAR IN charges the transmitter; the separate rear well stores it. Leaving the transmitter in the charge port while the processor is off slowly discharges it, so the storage well is the documented storage location. Wireless accepts Guitar,Wireless or Guitar+Wireless input selections; ordinary POD Go presets are compatible and their Guitar input is automatically converted to Guitar+Wireless when imported. USB3/4 is a reamp source; USB1/2 is monitoring that bypasses processing. Cable Tone and Wireless Gain are separate settings, not additional audio-effect blocks. PG-W<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** The [WirelessFAQ](https://kb.line6.com/pod-go-wireless-faq),rechecked2026-10-17,recommends resyncing in each new room: insert the transmitter intoGUITAR IN,wait **10–15seconds**,then reconnect to the guitar. Keep at least **3m** from2.4GHz Wi-Fi routers. Up to **four systems** are described with sequential scanning while earlier transmitters stay awake;after **four minutes** without audio the transmitter sleeps. These are conditional manufacturer instructions,not a venue reliability guarantee or floorboard battery rating. PG-W |

[Global1.12.5](https://cdn.eventideaudio.com/manuals/h90/1.12.5/content/system-menu/global.html); **Looper stereo:60s full-quality headline; speed table2x60s/1x120s/½x240s/¼x480s**, slower recording trades fidelity for length. Undo/redo, reverse, MIDI clock, one-button control; Punch/Repl-Punch unavailable in one-button mode. Max length only changes while empty. **Three HotSwitch parameter snapshots, one active at a time**; Perform mode supplies two assignment pages for the three physical footswitches. Drums unverified. [Parameters1.12.5](https://cdn.eventideaudio.com/manuals/h90/1.12.5/content/edit-modes/parameters.html),[Perform1.12.5](https://cdn.eventideaudio.com/manuals/h90/1.12.5/content/play-modes/perform.html) [Looper1.12.5](https://cdn.eventideaudio.com/manuals/h90/1.12.5/content/algorithms/looper.html) [P](https://www.eventideaudio.com/pedals/h90/)<br>**Processing / Looper1:** Looper<br>**H90 — firmware-qualified effect inventory:** Checked2026-10-17 against [manual1.12.5 index](https://cdn.eventideaudio.com/manuals/h90/1.12.5/content/algorithms/index.html). Counts derived from its named entries; **12+5+1+4+13+4+1+16+1+14+3=74**, plus two utilities outside the effect total. Lineage differs from H9’s grouping.<br>**H90 — firmware-qualified effect inventory:** The [looper page](https://cdn.eventideaudio.com/manuals/h90/1.12.5/content/algorithms/looper.html) retains its60s full-quality headline alongside the speed-dependent table. Do not infer120s at1x has the same fidelity as the headline. One-button control supports record/play/overdub, hold undo/redo and double-press stop; punch modes require dedicated transport controls. Stereo looper limits are not borrowed from H9 Max. |

## Why shared models are included

| Shared model | Retained bass evidence |
|---|---|
| [Sonicake Matribox II QME-100](SONICAKE.md#matribox-ii-qme-100) | Own Matribox II product explicitly lists guitar/bass/acoustic effects. Shared bass,not inferred from Pro or an EQ control. [Manufacturer](https://www.sonicake.com/products/matribox-ii). |
| [Sonicake Matribox QME-50](SONICAKE.md#matribox-qme-50) | Own original Matribox product explicitly supports guitar/bass/acoustic processing. Shared bass,not low-end performance certification. [Manufacturer](https://www.sonicake.com/products/matribox). |
| [Sonicake Pocket Master QME-10](SONICAKE.md#pocket-master-qme-10) | Own Pocket Master product explicitly lists guitar/bass/acoustic amp types. Shared bass;exact bass inventory/performance unresolved. [Manufacturer](https://www.sonicake.com/products/pocket-master). |
| [Sonicake Matribox II Pro QME-200](SONICAKE.md#matribox-ii-pro-qme-200) | Own firmware1.1.1 manual pp41,44 lists A BassVT/A BassFT bass amps and bass cabinets. Shared bass capability;not performance certification. [Official regional manual](https://www.hotone.jp/files/brands/sonicake/products/matribox-ii-pro/manual/20260803-070736-v5r131-matribox_ii_pro_jp.pdf). |
| [Sonicake Smart Box QME-20](SONICAKE.md#smart-box-qme-20) | Manufacturer Smart Box page explicitly describes presets optimized for guitar and bass. Shared bass capability; bass-specific algorithms and low-end performance remain unverified. [Manufacturer product](https://www.sonicake.com/products/smart-box). |
| [Donner Arena2000](DONNER.md#arena2000) | Own2022 Arena2000 manual p4 explicitly identifies input for guitar/bass;not connector-only inference. Bass-specific models/low-end performance remain unverified. [Original manufacturer manual](https://device.report/m/173567df81806b4e2da08b004d48be3265a7446aaebd91f140530baca984eb05_optim.pdf). |
| [Eventide H90](EVENTIDE.md#h90) | Own manual1.12.5 Global Source Type explicitly lists Bass(Tenor) and Sub(Bass), adapting pitch tracking, filters and tone controls. Not sibling or EQ-knob inference. [Manufacturer manual](https://cdn.eventideaudio.com/manuals/h90/1.12.5/content/system-menu/global.html). |
| [Eventide H9 Max](EVENTIDE.md#h9-max) | Own H9 User Guide p43 explicitly offers BASS and SYN.BAS source modes optimizing pitch algorithms and filter ranges; not inferred from an EQ knob or sibling. [Manufacturer manual](https://cdn.eventideaudio.com/uploads/2020/07/H9-Manual.pdf). |
| [Zoom 1201](ZOOM.md#1201) | Own1201 manual p4 explicitly covers electric guitar or bass through a preamp/compact effect;not direct high-impedance input. [Manufacturer manual](https://www.zoom.co.jp/sites/default/files/products/downloads/pdfs/E_1201.pdf). |

| [Behringer V-AMP 3](BEHRINGER.md#v-amp-3) | Manufacturer manual p8 documents two bass-preset banks, with recording-oriented qualification;not bass-specialist equivalence. [Source](https://mediadl.musictribe.com/media/sys_master/h56/hb7/8849514528798.pdf) |

## Scope and provenance

Instrument views overlap and must not be summed. All 297 active models count once in `research-state.json`; the six explicitly excluded archives never enter these tables. Former/donated eligible devices remain without private receipt/order/payment details. Root brand records take precedence where consolidated; legacy sources remain for full evidence until a checked lossless merge. No GitHub workflow is used.
